# per frame: seq | dt = frame pair time, Z = ToF range, dZ = range change over the pair
#   flow = frame motion at the centre (M7, RANSAC), px | inl = inliers / points
#   rot = gyro rotation over the pair, camera axes, deg | rotflow = what that rotation
#   moves the centre by, px (-fx*rot_y, fy*rot_x) | trans = flow - rotflow, px
#   v(tx) = -trans/f * Z/dt (check, from the centre only) | v cam = estimateVelocity,
#   camera axes | v world | pos = camera position, h = height | rpy = attitude, deg | acc, g
#   TRACK/COAST/LOST = camera / ToF state (measuring / missing a moment: IMU alone /
#   missing long: velocity pulled to 0), STILL = IMU sees no motion (velocity 0 if not tracking)
#   FUSED pos / v = the Kalman filters' output, +- = their 1 std | cam in = camera velocity
#   innovation (measured - predicted) / its std, then ok / REFUSED (outlier) / LATE / RESET |
#   tof in = ToF height innovation, mm (/ its std), ok / REFUSED / STEP (floor step) / RESET |
#   bias = accelerometer bias found, m/s^2
